For example a survival mod adds \"Decrease Movement -20 / -40 / -60\" for each stage of tiredness: while you are not tired they are all switched off and do not slow you; a lantern mod adds \"Is Daytime\" and \"Not Daytime\": only one of them is on at a time.\n\nWhen the condition becomes true (you get tired, night falls, you start sneaking...), the game switches the effect on by itself, and off again when it stops being true.\n\nA switched-off effect does NOT change any stat, speed or damage, so it is not a problem and is not \"stuck\".
